# Deployment SDK

> langsmith → Deployment SDK

(Formerly LangGraph Platform)

[![PyPI - Version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langgraph-sdk?label=%20)](https://pypi.org/project/langgraph-sdk/#history)
[![PyPI - License](https://img.shields.io/pypi/l/langgraph-sdk)](https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)
[![PyPI - Downloads](https://img.shields.io/pepy/dt/langgraph-sdk)](https://pypistats.org/packages/langgraph-sdk)

The Deployment SDK (`langgraph-sdk`) provides Python clients for interacting with LangSmith deployments. It includes clients for managing assistants, threads, runs, and cron jobs, as well as authentication utilities.

## Client

- [`get_client`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/_async/client/get_client) — Create and configure an async `LangGraphClient`
- [`LangGraphClient`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/_async/client/LangGraphClient) — Async client for the LangGraph API
- [`SyncLangGraphClient`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/_sync/client/SyncLangGraphClient) — Synchronous client for the LangGraph API

## Schema

- [`Assistant`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/schema/Assistant) — Represents an assistant with properties
- [`Config`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/schema/Config) — Configuration options for a call
- [`Interrupt`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/schema/Interrupt) — Represents an interruption in the execution flow
- [`Checkpoint`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/schema/Checkpoint) — Represents a checkpoint in the execution process
- [`StreamMode`](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/schema/StreamMode) — Defines the mode of streaming

For the full API reference, see the [langgraph-sdk package documentation](https://docs.langchain.com/python/langgraph-sdk/client).
